Passive and active querying are statistically indistinguishable; a matched-label-budget control attributes gains to deployment-time label production at 43% of exhaustive-review cost; a defect-memory extension fails decisively. Feedback is simulated from ground truth; live expert trials, where mislabelling is costliest on small banks, remain future work.

</details>


### [73] [Debate Training Reduces Reward Hacking in RLAIF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17776)
*Zachary Kenton,Lili Janzer,Rory Greig,Tian Huey Teh,Kirill Tyshchuk,Jonah Brown-Cohen,Harri Edwards,Senthooran Rajamanoharan,Noah Y. Siegel,Natasha Jaques,Rohin Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明了使用辩论（生成器和批评家之间的双人对抗博弈）进行强化学习微调，相比RLAIF基线能显著减少奖励破解问题，在数学任务上恢复了45%的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励破解是RLAIF的核心障碍：随着训练进行，策略学会利用AI评判器的系统性错误，导致任务性能下降。这一问题在评判器弱于策略时尤为严重，而这正是监督日益强大的AI系统最相关的场景。

Method: 使用Gemini 2.5 Flash级别的策略和冻结的更弱的Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite评判器，在可验证最终答案正确性的数学任务上，比较单人RLAIF基线与辩论方法。辩论采用生成器和批评家之间的双人对抗博弈结构。

Result: 基线方法快速破解评判器，而辩论在整个训练过程中保持评判器性能，达到更高的峰值验证准确率（恢复45%性能差距）。进一步弱化评判器会导致更快破解，但可通过增加辩论轮次补偿。批评家字数限制（有效上限150词）能成功平衡博弈并避免评判器破解。

Conclusion: 研究为辩论方法的可行性提供了积极证据，同时强调平衡多智能体训练的关键性：没有玩家约束时，对抗训练风险在于批评家默认转向评判器破解。批评家字数限制虽成功平衡博弈，但也通过限制批评家表达能力引入了权衡。

Abstract: We demonstrate that RL finetuning an LLM using debate, a two-player adversarial game between a generator and a critic adjudicated by a weaker LLM judge, reduces reward hacking compared to a reinforcement learning from AI feedback (RLAIF) baseline. Reward hacking is a central obstacle in RLAIF: as training progresses, the policy learns to exploit systematic errors in its AI judge, degrading task performance, a problem that worsens precisely when the judge is weaker than the policy, the setting most relevant to overseeing increasingly capable AI systems. We study mathematics tasks, where final-answer correctness is verifiable, allowing us to measure reward hacking dynamics. We train a Gemini~2.5 Flash-class policy with a frozen, weaker Gemini~2.5 Flash Lite judge, comparing a single-player RLAIF baseline against debate. While the baseline quickly hacks the judge, debate maintains judge performance throughout training, leading to a higher peak validation accuracy (45\% performance gap recovered) that persists through many RL steps. Additional experiments show that: 1) further weakening the judge leads to faster hacking, but this can be compensated by adding an additional debate round; 2) debate incentives override prompted misalignment; 3) RL using an LLM judge has a smaller train/validation reward gap than RL from verifiable rewards; 4) learning to critique to convince the judge using ground truth labels is possible but slow. Taken together, our results are a positive update on the feasibility of debate, while highlighting that balancing multi-agent training is critical: without player constraints, adversarial training risks defaulting to critic judge-hacking. We show that critique word limits (effective up to 150 words) successfully balance the game and avoid judge hacking, though this introduces a trade-off by restricting critic expressive clarity.

</details>


### [74] [Fourth-Moment Geometry of Rademacher Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17802)
*Peigan Gao,Jian Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了关于Rademacher和的高阶矩依赖性的问题，确定了p≥4范围内的高斯稳定性不等式以及p≥5时的最佳有限维L_p/L_4 Khintchine常数，证实了Jakimiuk等人提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Rademacher和的高阶矩如何依赖于四阶质量，并解决Jakimiuk以及Barański等人提出的关于高斯稳定性不等式和Khintchine常数的猜想。

Method: 采用四阶框架，结合固定的q-矩包络和凸性阈值以下的独立论证，利用Laplace变换形式给出系数敏感的尾界。

Result: 确定了p≥4全范围内的线性-q高斯稳定性不等式，证明了p≥5时的最佳有限维L_p/L_4 Khintchine常数（平坦系数向量为极值），并在p=3处证实了Jakimiuk猜想二次稳定性估计。

Conclusion: 所得界保留稀疏性和有效维度信息，可应用于Rademacher随机投影和随机符号误差，系数敏感的尾界具有实用价值。

Abstract: Let $\varepsilon_1,\ldots,\varepsilon_n$ be independent Rademacher signs and let $a=(a_1,\ldots,a_n)\in\R^n$ satisfy the normalization below. For the normalized Rademacher sum, we determine how its higher moments depend on the fourth-order mass. Combining a sharp fixed-q moment envelope with a separate argument below the convexity threshold gives the Gaussian stability inequality for the full range $p\geq4$ of this linear-in-q bound. The same fourth-order framework determines the sharp finite dimensional $L_p/L_4$ Khintchine constant for $p\geq5$, with the flat coefficient vector as the extremizer. These results settle the conjectures of Jakimiuk and of Barański, Murawski, Nayar, and Oleszkiewicz stated below. We also prove Jakimiuk's conjectured quadratic stability estimate at $p=3$. The resulting bounds retain information about sparsity and effective dimension, with applications to Rademacher random projections and randomly signed errors; those applications are not developed further here. Their Laplace-transform form also gives coefficient-sensitive tail bounds. The proofs are discovered with substantial assistance from ChatGPT 5.6 Sol.

</details>


### [75] [An Empirical Study of Reward Specification and Benchmark Reliability in GRPO-based LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17804)
*Rubén Balbastre,Juan Manuel Orduña,Mariano Pérez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM遗忘中未被充分定义的第三种行为：当提示词邻近目标但可以提供更广泛层面的答案时，模型应在该层面作答而非泄露、回避或拒绝。通过四种奖励设计对比实验发现，优化成功不等同于行为遗忘，不同评估方法会得出不同结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘研究关注两个目标：抑制目标特定知识和保持非目标效用，但在生成式QA中存在第三种行为未被充分定义——当目标邻近提示可以接受更广泛答案时，模型应该如何响应而不泄露目标特定信息、回避或拒绝回答。这一规范问题需要深入研究。

Method: 在控制性LoRA-GRPO RWKU设置下比较四种奖励设计：词汇抑制、反拒绝塑造、基于评分标准的广泛回答以及显式拒绝对比，同时考察有无SFT预热的影响。通过RWKU遗忘分数、保留完成审计、终端训练推出审计和训练动力学进行多维度评估。

Result: 实验表明优化成功不等同于行为遗忘：不同评估指标（RWKU遗忘分数、保留完成审计、终端训练推出审计、训练动力学）可能得出不同结论。这些分歧源于奖励劫持终点、GRPO中的策略支持限制、遗漏终点变化的基准探测，以及能在优化过程中选择语义泄露较低的广泛主题回答的奖励机制。

Conclusion: 研究发现LLM遗忘评估存在复杂性，传统优化成功指标不能完全反映行为层面的遗忘效果。需要更全面的评估框架来捕捉奖励劫持、策略限制和语义泄露等深层问题，以实现真正的行为遗忘而非仅满足数值优化目标。

Abstract: Practical LLM unlearning is usually evaluated through two objectives: suppress target-specific knowledge and preserve non-target utility. In generative QA, this leaves a third behavior underspecified: when a target-adjacent prompt admits a broader answer without target-specific leakage, the model should answer at that level rather than leak, evade, or refuse. We study this specification problem in a controlled LoRA-GRPO RWKU setting, comparing four reward designs that span lexical suppression, anti-refusal shaping, rubric-based broad answering, and an explicit refusal contrast, with and without SFT warm-up. The experiments show that optimization success is not equivalent to behavioral unlearning: RWKU forget scores, held-out completion audits, terminal training-rollout audits, and training dynamics can point to different conclusions. We trace these disagreements to reward-hacking endpoints, policy-support limits in GRPO, benchmark probes that miss endpoint changes, and rewards that can select broad-topic answering with low semantic leakage during optimization.

</details>


### [76] [MotoSafety: Edge-AI with Learned Temporal Importance for Two-Wheeler Collision Risk Assessment Under Time Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17823)
*Sumit S. Shevtekar,Chandresh K. Maurya,Gourab Sil,Subasish Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MotoSafety，一种用于摩托车碰撞风险评估的轻量级边缘AI架构，在94.97%准确率和0.135ms延迟下实现了高性能，适用于低成本硬件部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中低收入国家的两轮摩托车骑手面临严重的安全挑战，但关于时间压力等认知压力源如何影响碰撞风险的研究非常有限，缺乏大规模数据集和有效的评估方法。

Method: 构建了包含超过12.9万条多变量时间序列数据的大规模数据集，提出了基于学习时间重要性原则的MotoSafety边缘AI架构，使用时间压力作为归纳偏置，仅需1.15M参数即可实现高效部署。

Result: MotoSafety实现了94.97%准确率和99.33% ROC AUC，优于TimesNet和LLM4TS等10个基线模型；预测误差比Time-LLM和iTransformer低4.4倍；仅使用21个IMU+GPS特征即可达到93.91%准确率；在人体活动和临床领域分别达到97.66%和99.65%的准确率。

Conclusion: 该轻量级框架推动了摩托车碰撞风险评估的发展，支持智能交通系统的安全系统方法，具有良好的跨领域迁移能力和实际部署价值。

Abstract: Powered two-wheeler riders face critical safety challenges in low- and middle-income countries, yet limited studies exist on how cognitive stressors such as Time Pressure influence collision risk. To address this gap, we introduce a large-scale dataset of over 129,000 labeled multivariate time-series sequences from 153 simulator rides by 51 participants under No, Low, and High TP, capturing 64 features across vehicle dynamics, control inputs, proximity, and behavioral violations. Building on this dataset, we propose MotoSafety, a novel edge-AI architecture grounded in the Learned Temporal Importance principle. MotoSafety achieves 94.97% accuracy and 99.33% ROC AUC, outperforming ten baselines, including TimesNet and LLM4TS, and achieves 0.039 MSE and 0.094 MAE for forecasting (4.4x lower error than Time-LLM and iTransformer). With only 1.15M parameters and 0.135 ms latency, it is suitable for edge deployment on low-cost CPU hardware. Using ground truth TP as an inductive bias improves accuracy from 94.09% to 94.97%, while predicted TP achieves 94.82%. Using only 21 IMU+GPS features, it achieves 93.91% accuracy, indicating practical deployment. Beyond PTW safety, the architecture shows better transferability to human activity (97.66%) and clinical (99.65%) domains. This lightweight framework advances PTW collision risk assessment, supporting the Safe System Approach for Intelligent Transportation Systems.

</details>


### [77] [Leveraging Association Context Retrieval in Knowledge Edit- ing to Build White-Box Attacks on LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17836)
*Roman Maksimov,Vladimir Aletov,Vladimir Solodkin,Dmitry Bylinkin,Daniil Medyakov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于知识编辑的白盒攻击方法，通过定位-编辑框架和关联知识检索，成功诱导大语言模型产生不安全行为，攻击效果优于现有方法且保持模型一般性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型获得越来越多的自主权，研究如何诱导不安全行为变得至关重要。现有知识编辑方法会使模型对编辑目标分配异常高的预测概率，这一特性在攻击设计中具有独特优势。

Method: 提出一种新颖的白盒攻击方法，借鉴知识编辑领域的定位-编辑方法，并融入从模型检索的关联知识。该方法将约束移除扩展到整个主题类别，而非仅限于预定义数据集中的提示。

Result: 在多种架构上的实验表明，该方法相比竞争方法具有更高的攻击有效性，同时未对模型的一般性能造成重大损害。

Conclusion: 所提出的知识编辑启发的白盒攻击方法能够有效诱导大语言模型的不安全行为，为LLMs安全性研究提供了新的研究视角。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are granted increasing autonomy, it is essential to investigate methods that can induce unsafe behavior. We propose a novel white-box attack inspired by locate-then-edit approaches from the field of Knowledge Editing. Our choice is motivated by the observation that models edited with such schemes tend to assign unusually high prediction probabilities to the edit target, a property that is particularly advantageous when designing attacks. We modify the editing framework by incorporating as- sociative knowledge retrieved from the model, thereby extending constraint removal to an entire thematic category rather than being limited to prompts from a predefined dataset. Experiments with various archi- tectures demonstrate improved attack effectiveness over competing methods without dealing critical damage to general model performance.

</details>


### [78] [MoRAX: Mobility-based Representation Augmentation for Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17848)
*Ya Wen,Jixuan Cai,Yulun Zhou,Alec Kirkley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MoRAX框架，通过融合人类移动性数据增强地理空间基础模型，使其能捕获城市功能结构，并支持零样本跨城市迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型(GFMs)依赖地球观测数据，缺乏人类活动信息；而人类移动性数据虽能揭示区域间的功能关系，但通常局限于单一城市，难以用于可迁移的城市表示学习。

Method: 提出MoRAX框架，包含教师模型和学生模型：教师模型利用移动性数据通过调制机制将功能结构注入地理空间嵌入；学生模型无需移动性输入即可逼近教师性能，实现零样本部署。

Result: 在跨两个国家的四个目标城市上，MoRAX教师模型在八项社会经济与环境预测任务中持续超越GFMs和城市表示基线；学生模型在多数任务上接近教师模型性能；跨国迁移实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于移动性流条件的调制机制为地理空间基础模型融入城市人类维度提供了通用机制，实现了兼具覆盖一致性与功能结构信息的城市表示学习。

Abstract: Geospatial Foundation Models (GFMs) are emerging as a powerful paradigm for learning semantically rich and geographically consistent visual and physical representations. However, their reliance on Earth-observation (EO) data leaves information about human activity largely underrepresented. Human mobility data reveals the functional and relational structure between regions that is missing from EO data, but is often limited only to the city where it is observed, making it challenging to use for transferable urban representation learning. We introduce MoRAX, a lightweight framework for augmenting geospatial embeddings with functional structure derived from human mobility. MoRAX preserves the coverage and consistency of a GFM while providing information about the functional connectivity among urban regions, permitting zero-shot deployment in unseen cities with or without available mobility data. Across four target cities spanning two countries, the MoRAX teacher model, which observes mobility, consistently outperforms GFMs and strong urban representation baselines in eight socioeconomic and environmental prediction tasks. Meanwhile, the student model, which never takes mobility data as input, approaches the teacher in performance on most tasks. Transfer results across countries further demonstrate that modulation conditioned on mobility flows provides a general mechanism for grounding geospatial foundations in the human dimension of cities.

</details>


### [79] [Efficient Resource Optimization for Split Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17849)
*Wei Wei,Xianhao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对分割联邦学习(SFL)中的模型分割与资源分配联合优化问题，提出了一个多项式时间全局最优算法和一个具有(1+ε)近似保证的高效近似方法，有效解决了大规模用户场景下现有方案启发式或计算效率低的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分割联邦学习(SFL)中存在模型分割和资源分配的离散决策变量，导致混合整数优化问题。现有优化方案要么是启发式方法，要么计算效率低下，无法处理大规模用户群体，因此需要建立高效的优化框架。

Method: 首先研究模型分割问题，开发多项式时间全局最优算法；然后扩展到模型分割与资源分配联合优化问题，将其建模为二维主问题，并开发具有(1+ε)近似保证的高效近似方法。框架以最小化训练成本(延迟和能量成本的加权和)为目标。

Result: 开发的算法能够在多项式时间内求解模型分割问题的全局最优解；提出的近似方法具有理论保证，能够达到(1+ε)近似比；大量实验表明该方法能够有效平衡能耗和延迟。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一个高效的理论保证的SFL优化框架，成功解决了大规模用户场景下的计算效率问题，为资源受限网络中的SFL提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Split federated learning (SFL) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for model training at the edge. However, SFL inherently involves discrete decision variables for model splitting and resource allocation, resulting in a challenging mixed-integer problem. Consequently, prior optimization schemes for SFL are either \textit{heuristic} or \textit{computationally inefficient}, which cannot handle large-scale user populations. To address this limitation, this work establishes an efficient optimization framework for SFL under resource-constrained networks. Our framework jointly optimizes model splitting and resource allocation to minimize training cost, which is defined as the weighted sum of latency and energy costs. We first study the model splitting problem and develop a polynomial-time algorithm that achieves the global optimum. Then, we extend the approach to the joint model splitting and resource allocation problem. In this case, we formulate it as a two-dimensional master problem and develop an efficient approximation method with a $(1+ε)$-approximation guarantee. Extensive experiments show that the proposed approach provides efficient solutions to strike the optimal energy--latency tradeoff.

</details>


### [80] [Dynamic Compression in Recurrent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17896)
*Jyothish Pari,Ryan Bahlous-Boldi,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态压缩机制，允许循环模型选择性回顾历史并修正状态，在多函数存储任务中显著降低状态需求并提升扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统循环模型在单次因果遍历中压缩历史，必须在不知道未来使用方式的情况下压缩输入，导致固定大小的状态难以兼顾所有可能的未来需求。现有模型被迫以统一高保真度保留所有历史信息，造成状态容量压力。

Method: 提出动态压缩机制，允许循环模型选择性回顾过去的token并通过额外的循环更新来修正其固定大小的状态。模型无需在状态中以统一高保真度保留所有历史，而是在信息变得相关时从保留的原始序列中重新访问。在控制性实验设置中，模型先在上下文中学习多个函数，随后在序列后端遇到一系列少样本任务，需要识别并重用其中某个函数。

Result: 动态压缩显著降低了准确重用所需的循环状态大小；随着存储函数数量的增长，该方法相比单次遍历模型具有更优的扩展性能；证明了计算-内存权衡：循环模型可以通过更多计算来回顾历史，从而更高效地利用固定大小的状态。

Conclusion: 动态压缩突破了传统单次遍历循环模型的局限性，实现了计算与内存的有效权衡，为处理长上下文提供了新的架构思路。该机制使模型能够按需检索和精炼历史信息，而非被迫预先保留所有可能相关信息，为构建更高效的序列处理模型开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Recurrent models process long contexts efficiently by compressing their history into a fixed-size state, but modern architectures typically do so in a single causal pass over the sequence. Each input must therefore be compressed before the model knows how it will later be used, forcing a limited state to compromise across possible future demands. We introduce dynamic compression, which allows a recurrent model to selectively revisit past tokens and revise its fixed-size state through additional recurrent updates. The model need not preserve every part of the history at uniformly high fidelity in its recurrent state, because lower-fidelity information can be revisited from the retained raw sequence when it becomes relevant. We study this in a controlled setting where the model first learns multiple functions in-context and, later in the same sequence, encounters a series of few-shot tasks that each require it to identify and reuse one of those functions. A single-pass model must preserve every function at sufficient fidelity for any future task, whereas selective re-scanning allows the model to revisit and refine only the function currently needed. We find that dynamic compression substantially reduces the recurrent state required for accurate reuse and scales more favorably as the number of stored functions grows. These results demonstrate a computation--memory tradeoff in which recurrent models can spend more computation revisiting their history to make more effective use of a fixed-size state.

</details>


### [81] [Hybrid ML for Lightweight Pre-Route Delay Estimation in Open-Source IC Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17914)
*Marvin Castro Castro,Erick Carvajal Barboza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合决策树和线性回归的轻量级机器学习方法，显著提升OpenLane工具的布线前延迟估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态时序分析(STA)在数字集成电路设计流程中至关重要，但在缺乏物理设计详细信息时难以获得准确的延迟估计。

Method: 采用混合轻量级机器学习模型，将决策树与线性回归相结合，改善开源RTL-to-GDSII工具OpenLane生成的布线前延迟估计。

Result: 相比OpenLane的原始估计，误差降低80%；即使不使用OpenLane特定参数也能实现71%的改善；模型大小减少300倍以上，速度提升2倍。

Conclusion: 该方法为传统延迟传播技术和复杂机器学习模型提供了一种准确、高效且可解释性更强的替代方案。

Abstract: Static Timing Analysis (STA) is a critical step in the design flow of digital integrated circuits, however, obtaining accurate delay estimations can represent a challenge when limited information regarding physical design is available. In response, this work presents a hybrid and light-weight machine learning (ML) based approach that combines a decision tree with linear regression to improve pre-routing delay estimations generated by the open-source RTL-to-GDSII tool OpenLane. The proposed model achieves an 80\% reduction in error compared to OpenLane's estimates, demonstrates a 71\% improvement even without utilizing OpenLane-specific parameters. Overall, this method offers an alternative to traditional delay propagation techniques and more complex machine learning models that is not only accurate, but is also over 300 times smaller, 2 times faster and offers a higher explainability.

</details>


### [82] [Efficient RLVR Scheduling via Graph-Structured Online Difficulty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17941)
*Zhizhao Liu,Zhiliang Tian,Xi Wang,Zhihua Wen,Yihang Xiong,Zhiquan Lai,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了基于图的在线难度估计器，通过共享相关样本的反馈并持续更新难度估计，解决了RLVR中因样本难度不同导致的不高效探索问题，无需专用探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习验证奖励（RLVR）中，为不同难度的样本分配相同的探索预算效率低下：简单样本可能收到冗余的rollout，而困难但可学习的样本可能获得过少的探索。现有自适应调度器依赖于在线难度估计，但专用探测会带来大量生成开销，而历史估计器面临冷启动、反馈陈旧以及忽略样本关系等问题。

Method: 提出了一个即插即用的基于图的在线难度估计框架。首先，基于语义和推理相似性构建难度感知样本图；然后引入潜在难度状态并使用Potts先验鼓励相邻样本共享相同状态；接着采用状态级Beta-Binomial模型聚合与每个状态相关的rollout结果；最后使用在线平均场变分算法持续更新潜在状态分配和状态级难度。该框架无需专用探测即可集成到样本选择和rollout分配调度器中。

Result: 在多个基础模型、强化学习调度器和基准测试上的实验表明，该框架在无需专用探测的情况下实现了更优的性能。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过图结构和变分推断有效缓解了在线难度估计中的冷启动和陈旧问题，实现了难度自适应探索，提升了RLVR的效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models but relies on costly rollout exploration. Assigning the same exploration budget to samples with different difficulty levels is inefficient: easy samples may receive redundant rollouts, whereas difficult but learnable samples may receive too little exploration. Existing adaptive schedulers address this mismatch through curriculum-based sample selection or non-uniform rollout allocation based on estimated sample difficulty. However, obtaining reliable online difficulty estimates remains challenging: dedicated probing adds substantial generation overhead, whereas history-based estimators face a cold start with no initial observations and stale feedback, and typically ignore relations among samples. To address these limitations, we propose a plug-and-play graph-based online difficulty estimator that shares rollout feedback across related samples and continuously updates their difficulty estimates, mitigating cold start and staleness without dedicated probing. Specifically, we first construct a difficulty-aware sample graph based on semantic and reasoning similarities. Based on this graph, we introduce latent difficulty states and use a Potts prior to encourage neighboring samples to share the same state. We then employ a state-level Beta-Binomial model to aggregate the rollout outcomes associated with each state. Finally, we use an online mean-field variational algorithm to continuously update the latent-state assignments and state-level difficulty as new feedback arrives. Our framework can be integrated into sample-selection and rollout-allocation schedulers, enabling difficulty-adaptive exploration without dedicated probing. Experiments across multiple base models, RL schedulers, and benchmarks demonstrate that our framework achieves better performance.

</details>


### [83] [SIGMA: SHAP-Guided Implicit-Trajectory Generation for Metadata-Free LLM-Based AutoFE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17948)
*Xuan Zheng,Kento Uchida,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SIGMA框架，解决了LLM增强AutoFE在长周期优化中的可扩展性问题，在保持恒定提示词长度的情况下达到SOTA性能，并将生成特征的重复率从37.2%降至6.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的AutoFE方法在长周期优化中存在两大挑战：（1）语义元数据在许多实际场景中不可用；（2）轨迹累积会增加超出上下文窗口的风险，而缺乏轨迹会导致生成过程不稳定、陷入局部最优以及生成特征重复率高。

Method: 提出SIGMA框架，包含两个核心创新：（1）利用SHAP值替代语义信息，提供任务感知信号来指导组特征生成；（2）采用EXposed-feature Implicit Trajectory (EXIT)方法，通过提示词中暴露的特征隐式表示轨迹，实现恒定上下文长度的优化框架。

Result: SIGMA在接近恒定提示词长度下达到了与SOTA LLM基线相当的性能；EXIT将生成特征的重复率从37.2%显著降低至6.8%；SIGMA仅需平均5.4个特征即可匹配传统SOTA性能，展现出显著的特征利用效率提升。

Conclusion: SIGMA成功解决了基于LLM的AutoFE在可扩展性和适用性方面的关键限制，证明了在无语义元数据场景下，通过SHAP值引导和隐式轨迹表示可以实现高效、稳定且可扩展的特征工程优化。

Abstract: Recent research has leveraged Large Language Models (LLMs) to enhance Automated Feature Engineering (AutoFE) through semantic descriptions and trajectory-based prompting. However, there exist two challenges that limit their applicability and scalability in long-horizon optimization: (1) semantic metadata is unavailable in many practical settings, and (2) trajectory accumulation increases the risk of exceeding the context window, while without it, the generation process can become unstable, leading to becoming stuck in the local optima and a high duplicate rate of generated features. To this end, we propose a SHAP-enhanced Implicit-trajectory Generation for Metadata-free AutoFE (SIGMA), a scalable constant-context optimization framework. SIGMA leverages SHAP values to provide task-aware signals for guiding group feature generation instead of semantic information. In addition, we adopt an EXposed-feature Implicit Trajectory (EXIT) approach, where the exposed features in the prompt implicitly represent the trajectory. Empirical results demonstrate that SIGMA achieves performance comparable to the state-of-the-art (SOTA) LLM baselines with a nearly constant prompt length. Notably, EXIT significantly reduces the duplicate ratio of generated features from 37.2% to 6.8%. At the same time, SIGMA matches traditional SOTA performance with only 5.4 features on average, demonstrating substantial efficiency gains in feature utilization.

</details>


### [84] [An Omitted Mode Is a Rare Rule: The Sampling-Verification Danger Law in Continuous Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17956)
*Javier Aguilar Martín*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了Code World Model在连续控制中的认证局限性：接受仅证明样本一致性，无法保证规划器安全性。实验显示LLM在1D修复上表现良好(95/111)，但2D区域完全失败(0/156)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Code World Model范式下，LLM合成可执行的世界模型，经典规划器对其进行搜索，当模型重现采样转移时被接受。研究者质疑这种接受在连续控制中究竟认证了什么，以及存在何种安全隐患。

Method: (1)理论分析：定义管道危险度为期望风险，隔离精确因子——N个独立同分布门rollout全部遗漏概率为r的关键事件的概率恰为(1-r)^N；(2)边界定位预算证明：Lipschitz常数不超过L的模型在某点相差eta时，在体积至少为kappa((eta-eps)/L)^(d+m)的区域上差异超过容差eps；(3)实验验证：使用GPT-5.x进行真实LLM合成，在三个混合乐器上测试，包含1D钳位修复(111次模式包含抽取)和2D区域规则恢复(156个artifact)；(4)版本空间证书证明识别的类别相对性。

Result: (1)接受的模态盲模型被利用：规划器被钉在模态边界，遗憾接近整个可获得回报；(2)1D修复成功率：105/111次模式包含抽取中修复了遗漏的1D钳位，50/56个乐器流块上每次尝试都精确(95% CI [0.781, 0.960])；(3)2D区域完全失败：0/156个artifact恢复规则，8次针对性干预后失败仍存在；(4)类别相对识别：在最大剂量下20/20个块中声明的拟合成功，18/20中每个样本一致的圆都在容差内；(5)最终结论：对1034个artifact在独立样本上重新评分确认，接受仅认证样本一致性而无其他——在门可证明有信息量的地方，它仅覆盖被利用规划器查询的约2%。

Conclusion: 接受仅认证样本一致性，不能保证规划器安全性；存在一类入口规则与每个样本精确一致但在运行时无害，因此可识别性是仪器的可测量属性；版本空间证书证明识别是类别相对的。

Abstract: In the Code World Model paradigm an LLM synthesizes an executable world model that a classical planner searches, and the model is accepted when it reproduces sampled transitions. We ask what that acceptance certifies in continuous control. We define the pipeline's danger as an expected risk and isolate its exact factor: the probability that N i.i.d. gate rollouts all miss a critical event of probability r is exactly (1-r)^N; an independent acceptance sample adds its budget to the exponent. On three hybrid instruments the accepted mode-blind model is exploited: the planner is pinned at the mode boundary at a regret of nearly the whole attainable return. We prove a localization budget, valid at boundary points: models with Lipschitz constant at most L differing by eta at a point disagree above tolerance eps on a region of volume at least kappa((eta-eps)/L)^(d+m); the discontinuous reset modes studied pay no such budget. With real LLM synthesis, GPT-5.x repairs an omitted 1D clamp in 105 of 111 mode-containing draws -- every attempt exact on 50 of 56 instrument-stream blocks (95% CI [0.781, 0.960]). On 2D regions no artifact recovers the rule (0/156); eight targeted interventions leave the failure in place, and positive controls locate it: a located rule is not induced, while given form and location the constants follow exactly. A version-space certificate proves identification is class-relative: at the widest dose the declared fit succeeds in 20/20 blocks and every sample-consistent circle is within tolerance in 18/20. We prove a class of entry rules exactly consistent with every sample yet harmless at play, so identifiability is a measurable property of the instrument. Re-scoring all 1034 artifacts on independent samples confirms acceptance certifies sample consistency and no more: where the gate is provably informative it covers about two percent of the exploited planner's queries.

</details>


### [85] [Understanding the Surprising Generalization Properties of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17957)
*Nour Shaheen,Junwei Ma,Alex Labach,Frank Hutter,Valentin Thomas,Anthony L. Caterini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现仅对单个真实表格进行自监督预训练就能实现强大的迁移能力，并提出任务中心视角：特征数量而非实例数量决定表格质量，表格上下文泛化本质上是基于检索的过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格基础模型(TFMs)依赖大规模合成语料库或大量真实数据集进行训练，但对其预训练机制理解不足。研究旨在探索更高效的预训练范式，理解影响迁移性能的关键因素，并为模型和语料库设计提供理论指导。

Method: 采用自监督预训练方法，在单个真实表格上进行实验；通过系统性实验分析表格有用性的决定因素；提出任务中心的视角进行语料库设计；从检索角度重新理解TFMs的泛化机制。

Result: 发现单个真实表格的预训练即可产生强迁移能力；表格的有用性主要由特征数量而非实例数量决定；细粒度列级预处理能持续提升下游性能，而数据集级的过滤或去重无改善效果；表格上下文泛化本质上是基于检索的过程。

Conclusion: 提出了任务中心的表格预训练新范式，强调任务的数量和质量是TFMs预训练的核心；揭示了TFMs的检索式泛化机制，为未来模型架构和语料库设计提供了新的理论框架和实践指导。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) increasingly rely on in-context learning, where a model receives labelled examples at inference time and predicts labels for new inputs without updating its weights. Existing TFMs are typically trained on either massive synthetic corpora or very large collections of real datasets. In contrast, we show that surprisingly strong transfer can emerge from self-supervised pre-training on just a single real table. In this setting, we also find that tables tend to be either broadly useful or broadly poor regardless of downstream prediction task, and that the strongest predictor of usefulness is the number of features rather than the number of instances. This leads to a task-centric interpretation of tabular pre-training: the number and the quality of tasks are essential for the pre-training of TFMs.
  We show that the same task-centric perspective can help corpus design at scale: fine-grained column-level pre-processing consistently improves downstream performance, while no improvements are observed when we filter or deduplicate at the dataset level.
  Finally, we offer a new perspective for how TFMs generalize: we believe that tabular in-context generalization is largely retrieval-based, and good models are those that learn to identify relevant examples in the provided context and aggregate them well. The mechanics of TFMs have been relatively understudied; our task-centric, retrieval-based perspective offers a new framework to guide future model and corpus design.

</details>


### [86] [Too Sure to Be Safe: Model Calibration for Reliable Log Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17965)
*Bin Li,Dongdong Wang,Siyang Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对基于语言模型的日志异常检测器中存在的置信度校准问题，提出了LoRD框架，通过预测路径特定的重建距离来估计可靠性并选择性重新校准高风险预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于语言模型的日志异常检测器虽然在检测性能上表现优异，但其置信度估计存在严重的校准问题：在类别严重不平衡的情况下，检测器经常对错误预测赋予过高的置信度，尤其是对于异常日志；即便常规校准指标显示校准良好，错误预测的置信度仍然持续偏高，这在实际运维监控系统中造成了严重的可靠性隐患。

Method: 提出LoRD（Log Reconstruction and Distance）轻量级后处理校准框架：1）从正确分类的验证样本的潜在表示中学习预测路径特定的可靠性模型；2）通过路径维度的重建距离来估计预测可靠性；3）基于估计的可靠性，选择性地对高风险预测进行重新校准，以抑制过度自信的错误同时保留可靠的预测。

Result: 在四个大规模日志基准数据集和多个基于语言模型的检测器上进行了广泛实验，结果表明LoRD能够持续提升置信度可靠性，并显著减少与异常相关的过度自信错误，同时不影响异常检测性能。

Conclusion: LoRD框架有效解决了日志异常检测中的置信度校准问题，在不牺牲检测性能的前提下提高了系统的可靠性，为大规模计算系统的运维监控提供了更可信的异常检测方案。

Abstract: Online log anomaly detection is critical for maintaining the reliability of large-scale computing systems. Although recent language model-based log anomaly detectors achieve strong detection performance, their confidence estimates remain poorly calibrated. We show that these detectors frequently assign excessive confidence to incorrect predictions, particularly for anomalous logs under severe class imbalance. Moreover, confidence on erroneous predictions remains persistently high even when conventional calibration metrics indicate good calibration, creating a critical reliability gap for operational monitoring systems. To address this issue, we propose Log Reconstruction and Distance (LoRD), a lightweight post-hoc calibration framework for reliable log anomaly detection. LoRD learns prediction-route-specific reliability models from latent representations of correctly classified validation samples and estimates prediction reliability through route-wise reconstruction distances. Based on the estimated reliability, LoRD selectively recalibrates high-risk predictions to suppress overconfident errors while preserving reliable predictions. Extensive experiments on four large-scale log benchmark datasets and multiple language model-based detectors demonstrate that LoRD consistently improves confidence reliability and substantially reduces overconfident anomaly-related errors without sacrificing anomaly detection performance.

</details>


### [87] [Evaluating and improving crop-yield forecasting methods during extreme drought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17971)
*Shrey Gupta,Yi Ming,George Mohler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对极端干旱年份的玉米产量预测，比较了机器学习和深度学习模型的表现，发现深度学习模型VITA表现更优，但提出的样本加权和特征选择改进方法仅对非深度学习模型有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化对粮食生产的影响促使人们开发各种预测模型来识别气象驱动因素与作物生长之间的关系。极端干旱事件（如2012年美国中西部玉米带干旱）影响作物生产并考验这些预测模型的极限，本研究旨在评估这些模型在极端气候条件下的预测能力。

Method: 使用16个气象驱动因素作为预测变量，比较机器学习（非深度学习）和深度学习预测模型来预测县级玉米产量。针对训练集和测试集特征分布不相似（极端干旱年份的气象条件超出历史观测值范围）以及数据集的空间和时间不规则性问题，采用样本加权和特征选择作为改进方法。

Result: 改进方法对机器学习模型有提升效果，但对深度学习模型VITA几乎没有改善。无论是否进行改进，VITA模型的表现都优于机器学习模型。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了训练集和测试集特征分布不相似对预测模型的影响，比较了深度学习与非深度学习模型的性能，并提出了对非深度学习模型有效的改进方法，为极端气候条件下的作物产量预测提供了重要参考。

Abstract: The impact of climate variability on food production has led to the creation of various forecasting models that uses machine learning (ML), numerical weather predictors (NWP) or a hybrid of ML-NWP models to identify structural and physical relationships between meteorological drivers and crop growth, in order to predict crop yield. Droughts, for example the 2012 Midwestern US (Corn Belt) drought, are extreme events that affect crop production and test the limits of these forecasting models. Using 16 meteorological drivers as predictors, we compare ML (non-deep learning) and deep learning forecasting models to predict the county-level corn yield for the extreme drought year, 2012. This forecasting problem is characterized by a dissimilarity between the feature distributions of the training and test data, where the meteorological conditions of the extreme drought year fall outside the range of historically observed values. Additionally, the dataset consists of spatial and temporal irregularities where counties with missing yields introduce spatial sparsity and the use of only a subset of daily values per year introduce temporal sparsity. To overcome this, we use sample weighting and feature selection as modifications to improve our forecasting models. These modifications lead to an improvement for ML models; however, the deep learning model VITA shows little to no improvement. While VITA outperforms the ML models with or without modifications, our current study sheds light on the effect of dissimilarity between train and test feature distributions on forecasting models, compares deep learning versus non-deep learning models, and introduces modifications that are effective for non-deep learning models.

</details>


### [88] [Recirculation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17981)
*Michael C. Mozer,Shoaib Ahmed Siddiqui,Danny Sawyer,Sunny Sanyal,Rosanne Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为"循环"（recirculation）的推理时架构增强技术，无需训练即可显著提升现成基础模型的性能，在Gemma3模型上实现困惑度降低23%、GSM8k准确率提升21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈Transformer的状态更新受限于模型深度的基本限制，缺乏动态系统特性来跟踪信念状态。现有方法如链式思维计算复杂且适用于复杂推理而非基本状态跟踪，深度循环技术（循环）和训练循环Transformer成本高昂。

Method: 提出"循环"技术，引入特定形式的循环机制，使模型能够作为动态系统运作并跟踪信念状态。该技术在预填充阶段需要串行处理，但在生成阶段几乎无额外延迟。还提出了自适应变体，仅需轻量超参数调优而冻结原始模型权重，利用模型自身特性指导架构修改。

Result: 在Gemma3模型系列上取得显著提升：在多个数据集上困惑度降低23%，GSM8k准确率提升21%，并在其他下游任务上获得可靠改进。与现成基线相比实现了显著的性能增益。

Conclusion: 该无训练方法通过利用模型自身来指导架构修改获得成功，提出了一条由训练网络特性指导架构演化的路径，而非强制、任意的设计选择。这为现成模型的性能提升提供了低成本、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We describe an inference-time architectural enhancement for off-the-shelf foundation models that markedly reduces perplexity and boosts accuracy across generation and reasoning tasks. Our approach incurs essentially no additional latency during generation, though it requires serial processing in the prefill phase. Motivated by the fundamental limitation that state updates in feedforward transformers are bounded by model depth, our technique, recirculation, introduces a specific form of recurrence that allows the model to act as a dynamical system and track belief states. We distinguish this technique from chain-of-thought computation---which is better reserved for complex inferences rather than basic state tracking---as well as from popular depth-recurrence techniques (looping) and the costly training of recurrent transformers. We also propose and evaluate an adaptive variant of recirculation which requires only light tuning of hyperparameters while freezing the original model weights. Relative to the off-the-shelf baseline, adaptive recirculation achieves remarkable gains on the Gemma3 family, including a 23% reduction in perplexity on a suite of datasets, a 21% increase in accuracy on GSM8k, and reliable improvements in accuracy on other downstream tasks. Our training-free approach succeeds by leveraging the model itself to inform architectural modifications, suggesting a route to architectural evolution guided by a trained network's properties rather than forced, arbitrary design choices.

</details>


### [89] [Composing Flow-Matching Energies with Known Physics: Generation, OOD Detection, and Inversion on PDE Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18004)
*Yixuan Sun,Anirban Samaddar,Sandeep Madireddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过流匹配模型和势诱导速度获得显式标量能量的方法，解决了能量模型训练和采样困难的问题，实现了能量校正数据生成、OOD检测和逆问题求解三大应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率物理场建模需要结合数据驱动先验和物理结构（如控制方程）。能量模型(EBMs)因其能量可加性组合而成为自然选择，但存在训练和采样困难的问题，主要由于不可处理的配分函数。因此需要一种既能利用物理信息又便于训练和采样的方法。

Method: 提出使用流匹配模型(flow matching models)配合势诱导速度(potential-induced velocity)，在所有传输时刻产生显式标量能量。该方法：1) 能量梯度精确等于转换的学习分数；2) 在群体最优时恢复边缘负对数密度；3) 仅通过独立线性高斯插值上的匹配回归目标获得时间相关能量函数，无需变分形式或额外MCMC步骤；4) 采样保留流ODE特性。

Result: 显式能量函数实现三大功能：1) 能量校正数据生成，通过预测器-校正器采样框架中的通用MCMC采样器，相比流ODE基线降低PDE残差和谱距离；2) OOD检测中，数据能量与基于物理的能量(如PDE残差)作为互补机制提高检测精度；3) 逆问题求解中，通过将能量与二次观测似然组合得到后验能量，作为推理时目标的显式选择族。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将流匹配模型转化为具有显式能量的模型，既保留了流模型的采样优势，又获得了能量模型的可组合性，为物理信息增强的概率建模提供了有效框架，在数据生成、OOD检测和逆问题求解等任务中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Probabilistic modeling of physical fields benefits from both a data-driven prior and known physical structure such as the governing equations. Energy-based models (EBMs) are a natural fit since energies compose additively, which enables augmenting physics information during inference. However, EBMs have been difficult to train and sample from due to the intractable partition function. We show in this work that flow matching models with a potential-induced velocity yield an explicit scalar energy at all transport times, whose gradient is exactly the converted learned score and which recovers the marginal negative log-density at the population optimum. The time-dependent energy functions are obtained purely from the matching regression objective on an independent linear Gaussian interpolation, without a variational form or additional MCMC steps, and the sampling retains the flow ODE. Access to the energy function from a trained model serves three roles: energy-corrected data generation, energy as a scoring function for out-of-distribution (OOD) detection, and energy compositional posterior sampling for inverse problems. In particular, we show the explicit energy permits general MCMC samplers in the predictor-corrector sampling framework, reducing PDE residual and spectral distance compared to the flow ODE baseline. Furthermore, we demonstrate utilizing the data energy and physics-based energy (e.g., PDE residuals) as complementary mechanisms to improve detection accuracy for OOD tasks. In addition, we explore the connection to MCMC-based inference for inverse problems by composing the energy with a quadratic observational likelihood that yields a posterior energy, used as an explicitly chosen family of inference-time targets.

</details>


### [90] [Policy-Invariant Reward Shaping from LLM Feedback: A Framework for Hybrid RL Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18008)
*Christophe D. Hounwanou,John Emeka Eze,Yaé U. Gaba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将大语言模型与强化学习的混合架构形式化为目标增强马尔可夫决策过程，证明了将LLM的进度分数作为有界势函数使用时，即使LLM分数不准确也能保持最优策略集，并在小型MDP上进行了数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管将大语言模型与强化学习结合的研究越来越多，但LLM生成的奖励信号的理论地位往往没有明确说明。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为LLM作为奖励信号的混合架构提供形式化理论保证。

Method: 将混合LLM规划器和RL控制器架构形式化为目标增强马尔可夫决策过程。将LLM生成的每状态进度分数作为有界势函数，构建相应的奖励塑形项。理论分析证明该方法能够保持最优策略集。通过小型MDP上的四种势函数配置进行数值验证，包括一个放大到基础奖励幅度20倍的对抗性配置。

Result: 理论证明：当LLM的进度分数作为有界势函数使用时，由此产生的塑形项能够保持最优策略集，即使LLM分数不准确也是如此。这一保证比一般的"LLM作为奖励"方法更强。数值实验在小型MDP上验证了该结果，包括对抗性设置下也能保持性能。

Conclusion: 本文为LLM-RL混合系统提供了坚实的理论基础，证明了使用LLM进度分数作为势函数进行奖励塑形的理论可靠性，为实践中结合LLM和RL提供了更强的理论保证。

Abstract: Combining large language models with reinforcement learning is increasingly explored, yet the theoretical status of LLM-derived reward signals is often left implicit. We formalize the hybrid LLM-planner and RL-controller architecture as a Goal-Augmented Markov Decision Process and show that when the LLM per-state progress score is used as a bounded potential function, the resulting shaping term preserves the optimal policy set even when the LLM scores are inaccurate. This guarantee is stronger than what general LLM-as-reward approaches provide. We verify the result numerically on a small MDP under four potential configurations, including an adversarial one scaled to twenty times the base reward magnitude.

</details>


### [91] [Revisiting WEASEL 2.0: Reproduction, Sensitivity, and an Adaptive Ensemble-Size Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18021)
*Cian Higgins,Gerard Carrigan,Pinar Sungu Isiacik,Georgiana Ifrim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文复现了WEASEL 2.0时间序列分类器，验证了其性能，并发现最大集成规模规则对长序列数据存在过度配置问题，提出了自适应规则来优化内存和时间开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WEASEL 2.0中有两个超参数（最大集成规模和最大窗口大小）通过简单的阈值规则设定，但这些阈值缺乏经验性验证。本文旨在复现该算法并测试四个设计选择的敏感性，以验证其合理性并提出改进方案。

Method: 在114个UCR数据集上复现WEASEL 2.0算法，然后对四个设计选择进行敏感性测试：下游分类器、特征加权缺失、最大窗口大小规则和最大集成规模规则。针对发现的问题，提出了根据序列长度和类别数自适应设定最大集成规模的规则。

Result: 成功复现WEASEL 2.0（平均准确率0.865，中位数0.928，与已发表结果无显著差异）。发现最大集成规模规则对长序列数据过度配置，前三项设计选择对扰动具有鲁棒性。自适应规则在固定长度数据集上使峰值拟合内存中位数减少37MB（平均395MB），拟合时间中位数减少0.4秒（平均4秒），准确率变化中位数为0%（平均-0.11%）。

Conclusion: WEASEL 2.0的核心设计选择总体稳健，但最大集成规模规则需要改进。提出的自适应规则能有效减少内存和时间开销，尤其在长序列数据集上效果显著，且几乎不影响分类准确率。

Abstract: WEASEL 2.0 is a dictionary-based time series classifier that combines dilated sliding windows with a randomised hyperparameter ensemble and a fixed-size dense feature representation. Two of its hyperparameter choices, the maximum ensemble size and the maximum window size, are specified by simple thresholding rules whose chosen thresholds are not empirically justified in the original paper. In this work we reproduce WEASEL 2.0 on 114 UCR datasets, achieving a mean accuracy of 0.865 and median of 0.928, closely matching the published values (Wilcoxon signed-rank, p = 0.655). We then test the sensitivity of four design choices: the downstream classifier, the absence of feature weighting, the maximum window-size rule, and the maximum ensemble-size rule. The first three are robust to perturbation. The fourth is over-provisioned for long-series datasets, motivating an adaptive rule that sets the maximum ensemble size from series length and number of classes. Evaluated on fixed-length datasets, the adaptive rule reduces peak fit memory by a median of 37 MB (mean 395 MB) and fit time by a median of 0.4 s (mean 4 s), with a median accuracy change of 0% (mean -0.11%). Memory and time savings concentrate on long-series datasets where the original rule allocates the largest ensemble size.

</details>


### [92] [Why GPT-Style Models Do Not Directly Transfer to Symbolic Music: Compression in the Wrong Coordinate System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18025)
*Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“Effectiveness--Losslessness Framework”，将标记化重新定义为构建“预测有效且关系无损”的坐标系，并据此提出Fact–Token边界与Token–State边界；实验验证了有效坐标构建提升预测压缩性，保留上下文自由有助于高阶音乐结构的涌现，说明GPT式架构可迁移但标记化接口不能直接跨模态迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号音乐标记化借鉴语言模型的离散可复用单元思路，但仅关注可复用组合而忽视了压缩性；作者认为核心问题在于发现使音乐事实具有预测可压缩性的坐标系，而非寻找更大的音乐组合。

Method: 提出Effectiveness–Losslessness Framework：通过Predictive Effectiveness Principle界定Fact–Token Boundary（解耦与去嵌套构建坐标接口以暴露预测规律），通过Relational Losslessness Principle界定Token–State Boundary（在固定上下文依赖关系之前停止标记化，将其留给模型状态）；在受控符号音乐实验中验证这些边界。

Result: 有效坐标构建提升预测压缩性，固定关系投影会限制上下文建模；仅序列紧凑不等同于预测压缩，保留上下文自由可在无显式结构标签下涌现高阶音乐组织。

Conclusion: GPT式架构可跨模态迁移，但标记化接口需针对不同模态重新设计；标记化应在保留关系自由的前提下发现有效表示，以支持上下文结构的涌现。

Abstract: GPT-style models achieve strong performance by representing language with finite vocabularies of reusable discrete tokens. This success has motivated symbolic music tokenizations to treat recurring musical structures, such as chords, motifs, and phrases, as reusable units analogous to linguistic tokens. However, tokenization derives its advantage not from reusable combinations alone, but from compression: effective compression requires coordinates in which recurring regularities form stable and predictable conditional distributions. The key problem is therefore not to find larger musical combinations, but to discover the coordinate system in which musical facts become predictively compressible. We formulate the Effectiveness--Losslessness Framework and define tokenization as the construction of a predictively effective and relationally lossless coordinate system. The Predictive Effectiveness Principle defines the Fact--Token Boundary: decoupling and denesting construct coordinate interfaces that expose predictive regularities. The Relational Losslessness Principle defines the Token--State Boundary: tokenization stops before context-dependent relations are fixed, leaving their computation to model states. Controlled symbolic-music experiments validate these boundaries. Effective coordinate construction improves predictive compressibility, while fixed relational projections constrain contextual modeling. Sequence compaction alone does not guarantee predictive compression, while preserving contextual freedom allows higher-order musical organization to emerge without explicit structural labels. These results reveal why GPT-style models do not transfer directly across modalities: architectures transfer, but tokenization interfaces do not. Tokenization must discover effective representations while preserving the relational freedom from which contextual structure can emerge.

</details>


### [93] [TabNSM: Neural Sparse Mixer for Tabular Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18026)
*Ali Eslamian,Qiang Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TabNSM框架，通过自适应稀疏交互模块(ASIM)、多阶段回归头、GridLoss和RISE采样策略，在大规模高维表格回归任务中实现了优异的预测性能和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模高维表格回归面临两难困境：基于树的模型虽然鲁棒但缺乏端到端表示学习能力，深度模型虽然支持灵活特征学习但交互建模成本高且对噪声/冗余特征敏感，亟需一种兼具可扩展性和强表示学习能力的解决方案。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 自适应稀疏交互模块(ASIM)，集成前景特征发现、稀疏局部交互编码和特征-标记混合，在固定稀疏配置下实现近线性复杂度；(2) 多阶段回归头，用于渐进式预测细化；(3) GridLoss，一种融合目标结构的序数感知软分箱目标函数；(4) RISE，基于损失分位数区间的难度感知重加权实例采样策略。

Result: 在九个真实世界回归基准测试中，TabNSM展现出强劲的预测性能和实用性可扩展性，在高维和异构数据集上取得特别一致的提升，验证了选择性交互建模、结构化回归监督和难度感知采样的有效性。

Conclusion: 选择性交互建模、结构化回归监督和难度感知采样三者协同为深度表格回归提供了一种有效且可扩展的解决方案，为大规模高维表格数据回归任务开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Large-scale, high-dimensional tabular regression remains challenging: tree-based models are robust but lack end-to-end representation learning, while deep models enable flexible feature learning but often incur costly interaction modeling and sensitivity to noisy or redundant features. We propose TabNSM, a scalable regression framework that extends our earlier sparse-attention and mixer architectures. At its core, the Adaptive Sparse Interaction Module (ASIM) integrates foreground feature discovery, sparse local interaction encoding, and Feature-Token Mixing, providing near-linear complexity under fixed sparse configurations. For regression, TabNSM introduces three complementary components: a Multi-Stage Regression Head for progressive prediction refinement; GridLoss, an ordinal-aware soft-binning objective that incorporates target structure into representation learning; and RISE (Reweighted Instance Sampling by Error), a difficulty-aware sampling strategy based on loss-quantile bins. Across nine real-world regression benchmarks, TabNSM delivers strong predictive performance and practical scalability, with particularly consistent gains on high-dimensional and heterogeneous datasets. These results demonstrate that selective interaction modeling, structured regression supervision, and difficulty-aware sampling provide an effective and scalable approach to deep tabular regression.

</details>


### [94] [Optimize Your Sampling: Tuned Diffusion Sampling with Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18040)
*Travis Zhang,Christian Belardi,Justin Lovelace,Jin Peng Zhou,Saebyeol Shin,Carla P. Gomes,Kilian Q. Weinberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OYS方法，通过贝叶斯优化直接优化目标指标来选择扩散模型的采样时间步，在减少10倍推理成本的同时保留了89%-94%的50步质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型采样需要大量神经网络前向传播，计算成本高昂。现有研究主要关注高效求解器和采样器，但对采样时间步选择本身关注较少。近期方法优化理论推导的质量替代指标而非实际质量指标本身。

Method: 提出OYS（Optimizing Your Sampling）方法，将时间步选择视为黑盒优化问题，使用贝叶斯优化直接优化目标指标。该方法无需额外训练，适用于蒸馏模型，可改进Euler和DPM-Solver++等多种采样器。

Result: OYS在文本到图像生成上优于默认时间表和Align Your Steps方法，在图像修复等任务上优于默认时间表。定量和人工评估均表现优异。5步OYS时间表在减少10倍推理成本的同时，保留了50步时间表89%-94%的质量。

Conclusion: OYS方法通过直接优化目标指标的时间步选择，显著提升了扩散模型采样效率，在多种任务和采样器上表现优异，为扩散模型高效采样提供了新思路。

Abstract: Sampling from a diffusion model typically requires many forward passes through a large neural network, making generation computationally expensive. While much work has focused on efficient solvers and samplers, comparatively little attention has been paid to selecting the sampling timesteps themselves. A recent line of work optimizes theoretically derived surrogates for sample quality rather than the quality metric itself. We propose Optimizing Your Sampling (OYS), which instead treats timestep selection as a black-box optimization problem, optimizing the target metric directly with Bayesian optimization. OYS outperforms both the default schedules and those of Align Your Steps on text-to-image generation, and improves over the default schedules on inpainting and other image tasks, in both quantitative and human evaluations. OYS requires no additional training, is applicable even to distilled models, and improves both simple and sophisticated samplers such as Euler and DPM-Solver++. A 5-step OYS schedule retains 89%-94% of the quality of a 50-step schedule while reducing inference cost by 10x.

</details>


### [95] [The concentration game: Bayesian updating, regret, and information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18061)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个两人零和重复博弈框架，统一了贝叶斯更新与指数权重后悔值，揭示了一个普遍适用的后悔值分解结构，并将多种机器学习方法纳入同一理论体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后悔值分析方法（如方差代理、有界范围代理）是松散的松弛形式，缺乏统一的理论框架来解释贝叶斯更新、指数权重算法以及各类浓度现象之间的内在联系。作者旨在提供一个精确的理论框架，能够同时刻画贝叶斯推断与后悔值分析的本质。

Method: 作者设计了一个学习者与自然之间的两人零和重复博弈：终端收益定义为比较器在固定相对熵约束下相对于先验的最大增益，每轮约束为学习者的混合动作下自然移动的信息预算。通过贝尔曼方程分析，发现Gibbs/贝叶斯权重是唯一的贝尔曼均衡器——使每轮损失独立于自然移动方向的混合动作，对数配分函数扮演值函数角色。

Result: 后悔值可精确分解为三部分：反映观测结果变化的每轮信息损失、解释测量尺度变化的再回火漂移项、以及比较器相对于先验携带的信息量。此分解普遍成立，标准后悔值界中的方差和有界范围代理是其松散松弛。两位参与者的策略可从分解项逐项读出，重复博弈产生信息论账本替代传统的二次变分代理。

Conclusion: 同一比较器类别几何结构解释了经典大偏差界，且赌博机、后验采样、聚合、提升等方法都是同一后悔值分解的特殊情形。这表明贝叶斯更新与指数权重后悔值具有共同的博弈论根源，为理解各类机器学习算法提供了统一的几何视角。

Abstract: We give a two-player zero-sum repeated game between a learner and nature whose value identity generates Bayesian updating and an exact accounting of exponential-weights regret at once, and supplies the comparator-class variational form that a wide class of concentration phenomena share. The terminal payoff is the most a comparator can gain at fixed relative entropy from the prior, and the one-step constraint is an information budget on nature's move under the learner's mixed action. With the learner's move otherwise unrestricted, Gibbs/Bayes weights emerge as its unique Bellman equalizer -- the mixed action that makes the per-round loss independent of which direction nature moves -- with log-partition functions playing the role of value functions. The regret decomposes exactly into three parts: a per-round information loss reflecting the variation in observed outcomes, an additive retempering drift that accounts exactly for any change of measurement scale between rounds, and the information the comparator carries relative to the prior. The variance and bounded-range proxies that drive standard regret bounds are looser relaxations of this decomposition, which holds generally and governs them all. Both players' strategies are read off from the decomposition term by term, and repeated play yields an information-theoretic ledger of self-play in place of the usual quadratic-variation surrogate. The same comparator-class geometry accounts for the classical large-deviation bounds, and methods across bandits, posterior sampling, aggregation, and boosting are specializations of the one regret decomposition.

</details>

### [96] [An O-RAN-Assisted MARL Approach for Dynamic Sidelink and Infrastructure Selection in V2X Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17210)
*Maria Katarine Santana Barbosa,Kelvin Lopes Dias*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对6G车联网中直通链路通信引入的干扰问题，提出了一种基于Open RAN的多智能体强化学习（MARL）资源感知系统，通过智能选择传输模式来降低干扰并提升服务质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在6G车联网场景中，直通链路（SL）传输会导致车辆间以及车辆与智能交通系统其他实体间的干扰显著增加，从而降低车对车（V2V）通信和弱势道路用户（VRU）上行链路的性能甚至导致资源匮乏。现有解决方案主要集中在资源分配和配对选择优化，缺乏对通信模式和整个网络的全面考量。

Method: 本文利用Open RAN架构提供网络全局视图和开放接口框架，提出多智能体强化学习（MARL）资源感知系统。采用聚类策略降低系统复杂度，每个智能体管理一组通信对而非单个通信对。采用集中训练与分布式执行的架构，结合离线训练和离策略方法，通过经验回放进行微调，智能选择直通链路和网络传输模式以优化资源使用。

Result: 在纯车辆场景下，MARL方法使平均丢包率降低21%，时延降低19%。在VRU共存的混合场景中，与单智能体方法相比，丢包率和时延分别下降18%和30%。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于Open RAN的MARL系统能够有效缓解干扰、优化资源利用并提升服务质量，为6G车联网中的V2X通信提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Future applications in the 6G-based Internet of Vehicles will leverage sidelink (SL) transmissions in Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) scenarios. However, SL-based direct communication can significantly increase interference among vehicles and between vehicles and other entities of the Intelligent Transportation System. Thus, both Vehicle-to-Vehicle communications and Vulnerable Road Users (VRUs) uplink resources may be degraded or subject to starvation. Existing solutions primarily focus on improving resource allocation and pair selection. Nonetheless, they lack a comprehensive approach to tackle the communication modes and the entire network. To address these challenges, this paper leverages Open RAN to manage V2X communication and proposes a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) resource-aware system. Open RAN provides control loops through a global view of the network and also an open interface-based framework for machine learning models applied to resource decision-making. Meanwhile, the MARL model aims to mitigate interference, optimize resource usage, and enhance quality of service by optimally selecting between sidelink and network transmissions. To reduce system complexity, this work employs a clustering strategy. Each agent manages a group of pairs, rather than assigning one agent to each pair. The solution supports this design by adopting a centralized training with decentralized execution approach, empowered by Open RAN. The strategy uses offline training and an off-policy approach, in which each agent stores experience for fine-tuning. Results indicate that the MARL approach reduces average loss by 21% and latency by 19% in Vehicle-only scenarios. In coexistence VRU scenarios, loss and latency drop by 18% and 30%, respectively, compared to the single-agent approach.

</details>


### [97] [LoRIS: LoRaWAN-based IoT Platform for Sustainability Monitoring in Hotels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17467)
*Yash Pandey,Angus Gray,Reza Serati,Oscar Zhu,Emil Juvan,Anna Zinn,Danyelle Greene,Qingqing Chen,Sarah MacInnes,Siamak Layeghy,Sara Dolnicar,Marius Portmann*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LoRIS，一个基于LoRaWAN的物联网平台，用于酒店行业的可持续发展监测。该系统在澳大利亚和斯洛文尼亚的21个酒店场所部署了850个传感器，成功解决了酒店运营中资源消耗监测的数据精度问题，同时保障了客人隐私和体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 酒店业是全球温室气体排放、水资源压力和废物产生的主要来源，但酒店的可持续发展报告仍受限于粗糙的手动收集运营数据。缺乏高分辨率的资源消耗、环境条件和客人行为数据，限制了对酒店可持续性表现的准确评估和改进。

Method: 提出了LoRIS（LoRaWAN-based IoT platform for sustainability monitoring in hotels）系统，采用LoRaWAN参考模型架构。系统设计考虑了酒店部署的运营现实：严格的酒店IT政策、客人隐私期望、快速可逆的安装以及多年电池运行。采用隐私保护设计指导传感器模态选择和部署分区，端到端加密保护从传感器到仪表板的数据传输。系统采用托管无服务器基础设施处理数据。

Result: 系统自2022年2月运行至今，跨越澳大利亚和斯洛文尼亚21个场所，部署了19种类型的850个传感器，覆盖AU915和EU868两个监管区域。平台已生成超过2.02亿条传感器记录，每天摄取约24.5万条上行消息。系统成功用于7项涉及食品浪费、能源消耗和水资源消耗的实地研究，包括测量环境结果和客人满意度的对照干预实验。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了LoRaWAN传感技术可以在不损害客人体验或隐私的前提下，在运营酒店中大规模部署。系统为酒店行业的可持续发展监测提供了可行的技术解决方案，支持精准的资源消耗数据收集和环境行为研究。

Abstract: The hospitality sector is a major source of global greenhouse gas emissions, water stress, and waste generation, yet sustainability reporting in hotels remains constrained by coarse, manually collected operational data. We present LoRIS (LoRaWAN-based IoT platform for sustainability monitoring in hotels), a LoRaWAN-based sensing system that delivers high-resolution measurements of resource consumption, environmental conditions, and guest behaviour across geographically distributed hotel properties. The architecture follows the canonical LoRaWAN reference model and is built for the operational realities of hospitality deployments: restrictive hotel IT policies, guest privacy expectations, rapid and reversible installation, and multi-year battery operation. Privacy-by-design guides modality selection and deployment zoning, and end-to-end encryption protects data from sensor to dashboard. This system has been running since February 2022 and currently spans 850 sensors of 19 types across 21 sites in Australia and Slovenia, covering both the AU915 and EU868 regulatory regions. The platform has generated over 202 million sensor records and ingests approximately 245,000 uplink messages per day on managed serverless infrastructure. Our system has been successfully used for seven field studies spanning food waste, energy consumption, and water consumption, including controlled intervention experiments that measure environmental outcomes and guest satisfaction in parallel. This system shows that LoRaWAN sensing can be deployed at scale in operational hotels without compromising guest experience or privacy.

</details>


### [98] [Predict Before Replay: Joint FEC and Flight Control for Reliable Scale-Up Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17503)
*Fan Yang,Jiaqi Liu,Tao Jiang,Zhan Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出PREFACE方法，通过预FEC控制器处理时相关突发错误，显著提升高速链路的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数百Gbps的高速串行链路中，传统的可靠性管道（FEC+重传）面临严重问题：延迟反馈导致后续数据包在残留故障报告前进入重传窗口，标准重传机制会将单个损坏数据包放大为后缀重传，导致性能显著下降。

Method: 提出PREFACE预FEC控制器，采用两状态贝叶斯滤波器将已纠正符号观测转换为下一数据包的突发后验概率，并联合选择FEC强度与未确认数据包上限。在ns-3中实现，遵循公开可验证的UALink 200G 1.0重传语义。

Result: PREFACE将有效吞吐量提升10.52%，P99延迟降低50.75%，重传减少47.52%，建模的环AllReduce性能提升13.1-27.0%。

Conclusion: PREFACE通过预测性FEC控制有效解决了高速链路中时相关突发错误导致的重传放大问题，在多个关键性能指标上均取得显著改善。

Abstract: Scale-up accelerator fabrics send latency-sensitive flits over serial links at hundreds of gigabits per second. Their reliability pipeline first relies on FEC, then detects residual failures and replays unacknowledged data. At these line rates, delayed feedback lets later flits enter the replay window before a residual failure is reported, so standard replay can amplify one corrupted flit into a suffix retransmission. This paper presents PREFACE, a pre-FEC controller for temporally correlated burst errors. A two-state Bayesian filter converts corrected-symbol observations into a next-flit burst posterior and jointly selects FEC strength with an outstanding-flit cap. We implement PREFACE in ns-3 with publicly verifiable UALink 200G 1.0 replay semantics. PREFACE improves goodput by 10.52%, lowers P99 latency by 50.75%, cuts replay by 47.52%, and improves modeled ring AllReduce by 13.1--27.0%.

</details>


### [99] [Completion-Path Credits: Multi-Resource Control for Scale-Up Fabrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17523)
*Fan Yang,Jiaqi Liu,Tao Jiang,Zhan Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出SemaCredit接收端控制器，通过细粒度的多资源信号量信用机制替代传统字节信用，有效解决了小操作在原子操作和响应注入竞争下的高延迟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连接GPU和AI加速器的扩展架构网络中，传统的以字节为单位的信用机制（Byte-denominated credits）仅关注链路缓冲区和HBM流量保护，但无法准确表征以原子操作执行或响应注入为主导的小操作的资源需求，导致小操作在资源竞争下遭受严重延迟惩罚。

Method: 提出SemaCredit接收端控制器，核心机制是：针对每个远程内存操作，在执行前检查其向量化的目标资源需求（包括HBM、原子操作引擎、响应引擎），并为每个操作分配多维度资源信用；当操作的某个阶段（HBM访问、原子执行或响应生成）完成后，即时释放对应的资源信用分量。

Result: 在包含多路径队列、8个HBM分区、串行化原子引擎和响应引擎的确定性事件模拟器中：1）在原子竞争场景下，小操作P99延迟降低52.4%；2）在响应incast场景下，小操作P99延迟降低10.2%；3）在AllReduce型和远程读取型流量混合场景下，P99延迟分别改善57.7%和14.5%，同时在HBM主导的MoE流量上保持与字节信用机制相当的性能。

Conclusion: SemaCredit通过细粒度的多资源感知信用管理，在保持HBM重负载场景性能的同时，显著提升了小操作在原子操作和响应竞争下的性能，为AI加速器网络中的资源管理提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Scale-up fabrics connecting GPUs and AI accelerators carry tensor transfers together with remote reads, writes, atomics, and notifications over shared target-side receiver resources. Byte-denominated credits protect link buffers and streaming HBM traffic, but poorly represent small operations dominated by Atomic execution or response injection. This paper presents SemaCredit, a receiver controller that admits each remote-memory operation against a vector of target-resource demands and returns each component when its corresponding HBM, Atomic, or response stage completes. In a deterministic event simulator with multipath queues, eight HBM partitions, a serialized Atomic engine, and a response engine, SemaCredit matches a strong per-resource byte baseline on HBM-hotspot traffic while reducing small-operation P99 latency by 52.4% under Atomic contention and 10.2% under response incast. Application-shaped mixes show 57.7% and 14.5% P99 latency improvements for AllReduce-shaped and remote-read-shaped traffic while matching byte credits on HBM-dominated MoE traffic.

</details>


### [100] [Array-Based Molecular Pulse Encoding for Neuro-Spike Communication in Intra-Body Nano-networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17675)
*Keyvan Aghababaiyan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经尖峰阵列的通信方案，通过分子脉冲序列编码信息消除了严格时间同步需求，通信速率提升75%-150%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经尖峰通信系统依赖精确的时间同步来编码信息，这对资源受限的纳米机器构成了重大挑战。由于天然神经尖峰通信通常依赖瞬时尖峰率和时间间隔来传递信息，这种时间编码方案需要发射器和接收器之间的精确时间同步，因此在未来的体内纳米网络中迫切需要开发能够在降低同步要求（如符号同步）下运行的通信方案。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的基于神经尖峰阵列的通信方案，通过纳米机器发射的不同分子脉冲的特定排列来编码信息。该方案通过分子脉冲发射的序列而非精确时间来区分符号，从而消除了严格时间同步的需求。通过理论分析推导了符号间干扰概率、错误概率和可实现的通信速率的表达式。

Result: 分析和数值结果表明，该阵列方案在各种扩散系数下显著优于先前提出的符号同步模型，通信速率提升了75%至150%。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于阵列的神经尖峰通信方案成功解决了资源受限纳米机器的时间同步挑战，在保持通信可靠性的同时显著提高了通信效率，为未来体内纳米网络的通信设计提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate a neuro-spike communication system designed to bridge severed connections between damaged neurons using auxiliary nano-machines. Natural neuro-spike communication typically relies on instantaneous spike rates and temporal intervals to convey information. However, these temporal encoding schemes require exact time synchronization between the transmitter and receiver, a requirement that poses a significant challenge for resource-constrained nano-machines. To address this issue, it is imperative for future intra-body nano-networks to develop communication schemes that operate under reduced-order synchronization (e.g., symbol-synchronized). In this paper, we propose a novel neuro-spike array-based communication scheme where information is encoded through the specific arrangement of distinct molecular pulses emitted by nano-machines. By distinguishing symbols based on the sequence of these emissions rather than their exact timing, the need for stringent time synchronization is eliminated. We theoretically analyze the performance of the proposed scheme by deriving expressions for the probability of inter-symbol interference (ISI), error probability, and the achievable communication rate. Analytical and numerical results demonstrate that our array-based scheme significantly outperforms previously proposed symbol-synchronized models, providing a 75% - 150% enhancement in the communication rate across various diffusion coefficients.

</details>


### [101] [Threat Aware Task Offloading and Caching for Secure UAV Assisted Vehicular Consumer Electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17794)
*Xiaoteng Yang,Sunil Prajapat,Zheng Lin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无人机辅助的车载边缘计算架构，通过威胁感知的任务卸载和智能时空缓存机制，有效降低了任务延迟并提高了缓存效率，解决了动态车联网环境下的安全和效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载消费电子产品对计算密集型和延迟敏感型服务的需求日益增长，这给车载边缘计算系统带来了严格的效率、可靠性和安全性要求。在动态的车载环境中，基于推理的信息泄露和异常通信行为进一步威胁着系统性能和数据隐私。

Method: 提出了一种无人机辅助的协作式车载边缘计算架构，整合了威胁感知的任务卸载和跨路侧单元及无人机边缘节点的智能时空缓存。构建了安全感知的上行链路传输模型以捕获潜在的信息泄露风险和异常通信模式，并提出了威胁感知联合优化（TAGO）框架，该框架结合了近端策略优化算法进行自适应任务卸载，以及基于Frank-Wolfe算法的梯度缓存更新来捕获时空服务流行度。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与多种基线策略相比，该方法显著降低了任务延迟并提高了缓存效率，验证了其在安全和高效的无人机辅助车载消费电子系统中的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的无人机辅助车载边缘计算架构及威胁感知联合优化框架能够有效应对动态车载环境中的安全和效率挑战，为车载消费电子系统提供了一种安全高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vehicular consumer electronics increasingly support computation-intensive and latency-sensitive services, imposing stringent efficiency, reliability, and security requirements on vehicular edge computing (VEC) systems. In dynamic vehicular environments, inference-based information leakage and anomalous communication behaviors further threaten system performance and data privacy. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a UAV-assisted cooperative VEC architecture that integrates threat-aware task offloading with intelligent spatiotemporal caching across roadside units (RSUs) and UAV edge nodes. A security-aware uplink transmission model is developed to capture potential information leakage risks and abnormal communication patterns, enabling adaptive offloading decisions. We formulate a joint optimization problem to minimize end-to-end task execution delay while improving cache utilization under limited computing and storage resources. To efficiently solve this problem, a Threat-Aware Joint Optimization (TAGO) framework is designed by combining proximal policy optimization for adaptive task offloading and a gradient-based caching update derived from the Frank-Wolfe algorithm to capture spatiotemporal service popularity. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly reduces task delay and improves cache efficiency compared with several baseline strategies, showing its effectiveness for secure and efficient UAV-assisted vehicular consumer electronics systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [WONDER: A Radio World Model-based Negotiation Framework for Multi-Agent UAV Coverage Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16955)
*Jiahao Huang,Rongpeng Li,Zhifeng Zhao,Guoru Ding,Honggang Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了WONDER框架，利用基于JEPA的无线电世界模型和分布式多轮协商机制，解决灾后无人机群快速恢复无线覆盖时面临的局部观测与全局覆盖效果差距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后地面基础设施受损导致无线覆盖中断，无人机群可快速恢复覆盖，但存在局部几何观测受限、无线电影响不可见、无人机间通信受限等问题，导致局部可见移动选择与集群级覆盖效果之间存在显著差距。

Method: 提出WONDER框架：1）采用基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的无线电世界模型，从部署可用信息学习并预测候选轨迹的增量无线电效应；2）通过多轮协商协调排序提案，每次提交一条轨迹并在更新上下文下重新评估剩余提案；3）采用近端策略优化（PPO）风格的Actor，交替更新世界模型和Actor；同时建立了RadioDynamics仿真环境，整合无人机移动性、无线电传播、无人机间通信建模和数字孪生几何。

Result: 在RadioDynamics的11个测试场景中，WONDER在七种评估方法中获得最高平衡分数0.870，相比STACCA具有0.162的覆盖优势，同时保持100%的无人机间连通性。

Conclusion: 基于无线电世界模型的优化协商框架能有效弥合局部观测与全局覆盖之间的差距，为灾后无人机群无线覆盖恢复提供了一种有效的分布式解决方案。

Abstract: Post-disaster damage to terrestrial infrastructure can disrupt wireless coverage,while Uncrewed Aerial Vehicle (UAV) swarms provide a promising solution for rapid restoration.However, due to the limitations in local geometry observations hidden radio impact,and inter-UAV communication,there exists a significant gap between locally visible movement choices and swarm-level coverage outcomes.To combat this gap,we propose a raido World-model-based Optimized Negotiation framework for Distributed UAV covERage (WONDER).Particularly, to tackle the unavailability of the future radio field from onboard observations, WONDER uses a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA)-based radio world model to learn and predict the incremental radio effect of each candidate trajectory from deployment-available information.Multi-round negotiation in WONDER then coordinates ranked proposals by committing one trajectory at a time and re-evaluating the remaining proposals under the updated context. Our theoretical analyses further validate the effectiveness of such a world model-based framework. WONDER also adopts a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)-style Actor and alternates between updating the world model and the actor. Furthermore,we build RadioDynamics,a comprehensive simulation environment that integrates UAV mobility,radio propagation, inter-UAV communication modeling,and digital-twin geometry with ray-traced fields in $62$ metropolitan scenes.Experiments on $11$ testing scenes in RadioDynamics show that WONDER achieves the highest balanced score among seven evaluated methods,reaching $0.870$ with a $0.162$ coverage advantage over STACCA, while maintaining $100\%$ connectivity between UAVs.

</details>


### [103] [Adaptive Incentive Design in Dynamic Principal-Agent Problem via Kernelized Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17614)
*Arghya Mallick,Anuj S. Vora,Sergio Grammatico,Peyman Mohajerin Esfahani*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 通过引入随机效用模型解决了动态委托-代理问题中期望效用不连续的瓶颈，将其建模为异方差多臂老虎机问题，提出了Heteroscedastic GP-UCB算法并证明了$O(\sqrt{T}(\log T)^{m+1})$的后悔界，在V2G激励设计中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态委托-代理文献假设代理效用是确定性的，导致委托人期望效用不连续，迫使对合同空间进行计算上难以处理的离散化处理，这是一个根本性瓶颈。

Method: 1) 在代理效用模型中引入随机成分以恢复期望效用的连续性；2) 将交互建模为具有异方差噪声的结构化多臂老虎机问题；3) 提出使用神经网络(Arcsin)核的Heteroscedastic GP-UCB算法，以捕捉效用景观的非平稳、S型几何结构。

Result: 对于m维紧致合同空间，建立了高概率累积后悔界$O(\sqrt{T}(\log T)^{m+1})$；在V2G激励设计问题中验证了框架的实用性，证明了其与动态委托-代理问题的等价性，并展示了电网聚合商卓越的经济性能。

Conclusion: 随机效用建模成功恢复了期望效用的连续性，使得基于连续几何结构的高效在线学习算法成为可能，为解决动态委托-代理问题提供了新的理论框架和实践方案。

Abstract: We consider the dynamic principal-agent problem under asymmetric information, wherein a principal sequentially designs contracts to incentivize an agent with unknown preferences and hidden actions. A fundamental bottleneck in the existing literature is the assumption of deterministic agent utility, which renders the principal's expected utility discontinuous and forces computationally intractable discretizations of the contract space. In this paper, we address this limitation by introducing a stochastic counterpart into the agent's utility model, capturing the inherent physical and behavioral variations in realistic subsystems. We formally prove that this stochastic formulation restores the continuity of the principal's expected utility. Leveraging this continuous geometric structure, we formulate the interaction as a structured multi-armed bandit problem subject to heteroscedastic noise. We propose a \texttt{Heteroscedastic GP-UCB} algorithm that utilizes a Neural Network (Arcsin) kernel, chosen to capture the non-stationary, sigmoidal geometry of the utility landscape. For an $m$-dimensional compact contract space, we establish a high-probability cumulative regret bound of $O\left(\sqrt{T}(\log T)^{m+1}\right)$. Finally, we demonstrate the practical efficacy of our theoretical framework by formulating the Vehicle-to-Grid (V2G) incentive design problem, proving its equivalence to a dynamic principal-agent problem, and showing superior economic performance for grid aggregators.

</details>


### [104] [Offline Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning with a Physics-Informed World Model for Cooperative Mixed Traffic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17739)
*Lu Liu,Chi Xie,Xi Xiong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于物理信息世界模型的离线多智能体强化学习框架，用于解决部分可观测高速公路瓶颈处的网联自动驾驶车辆协同控制问题，无需依赖全局交通状态或在线试错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合交通流中，网联自动驾驶车辆（CAVs）协同控制以缓解高速公路瓶颈拥堵面临关键挑战：无法获取完整的全局交通状态，且在线试错方法在实际应用中存在安全风险和效率问题。

Method: 提出物理信息世界模型框架：利用局部CAV观测-动作历史重构物理可解释的全球交通状态；结合宏观-微观交通动力学提供物理监督；采用概率集成世界模型学习交通状态转移和系统奖励，并通过模型分歧量化认知不确定性；使用悲观奖励和不确定性驱动截断的多步想象展开进行离线策略学习。

Result: 在基于SUMO的匝道瓶颈实验中，利用约100万条离线转换数据验证表明：物理监督显著提升了状态重构精度和世界模型预测准确性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了在不依赖全局信息和在线学习的前提下，有效缓解混合交通流中的高速公路瓶颈拥堵，为CAV协同控制提供了数据高效且物理可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: This study investigates cooperative control of connected and automated vehicles (CAVs) at partially observable highway bottlenecks in mixed traffic, aiming to mitigate congestion without relying on complete global traffic states or online trial-and-error. We propose a physics-informed world model-based offline multi-agent reinforcement learning framework that reconstructs a physically interpretable global traffic state from local CAV observation-action histories, with coupled macroscopic-microscopic traffic dynamics providing physics-based supervision. A probabilistic ensemble world model learns traffic-state transitions and system rewards, while model disagreement quantifies epistemic uncertainty. Multi-step imagined rollouts with pessimistic rewards and uncertainty-driven truncation are then used for offline policy learning. Experiments in a SUMO-based on-ramp bottleneck using approximately $1\times10^6$ offline transitions show that physics supervision improves state reconstruction and world-model prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [105] [A Theoretical Framework for Parallel Lifelong MAPF Using Group Decentralized Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17928)
*Alex DeWeese,Jiaoyang Li,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文证明了RHCR算法在L-MAPF问题中的近最优性，并提出了GD-RHCR框架，通过分组并行规划降低计算成本，同时保持理论上的近最优保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RHCR框架虽然是L-MAPF问题的优秀解决方案，但其高昂的计算成本限制了其在中等规模智能体数量场景下的应用。为了解决这一计算瓶颈，作者希望找到既能降低计算成本又能保持解质量的方法。

Method: 作者首先利用Locally Interdependent Multi-Agent MDP文献中的理论方法，在折扣MDP形式化下证明了RHCR的近最优性。然后基于这些理论结果，提出GD-RHCR框架，该框架引入分组去中心化结构：通过传递式通信方案将智能体进行分区，并对每个分区的智能体进行并行规划。作者建立了RHCR基于时间的限制与GD-RHCR基于空间的分区之间的理论对偶性。

Result: 理论结果表明，RHCR和GD-RHCR都能达到类似的指数级接近最优的保证。实验结果表明，在不同地图上，GD-RHCR能够实现高吞吐量，能够扩展到更高数量的智能体，同时显著降低每次规划的计算成本。

Conclusion: 该工作成功地将空间分区机制融入RHCR框架，实现了计算效率与解质量之间的良好平衡，证明了基于时间的限制与基于空间的分区之间的理论对偶性，为大规模L-MAPF问题提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: In the Lifelong Multi-Agent Path Finding (L-MAPF) problem, agents must repeatedly move from one destination to another while avoiding obstacles and inter-agent collisions. Widely regarded as one of the highest-performing solutions to this problem is the Rolling-Horizon Collision Resolution (RHCR) framework. However, commensurate with its quality solutions, it incurs a computational cost that limits its applicability to even modest agent counts. In this paper, leveraging theoretical methods from the Locally Interdependent Multi-Agent MDP literature, we first theoretically prove the near-optimality of RHCR in a discounted MDP formulation of the L-MAPF problem. Then, we leverage these results to naturally motivate an extended framework called Group Decentralized RHCR (GD-RHCR) which incorporates a group decentralized structure that partitions agents based on a transitive communication scheme and plans for each partition of agents in parallel. We show that both RHCR and GD-RHCR achieve similar exponentially close to optimal guarantees, establishing a theoretical duality between the time based restrictions performed by vanilla RHCR and the additional space based partitioning performed by GD-RHCR. Lastly, we show that across varying maps, GD-RHCR is able to attain high throughput that scales into higher agent counts while maintaining a significantly lower per plan cost.

</details>


### [106] [Collective Counterfactual Planning: Coordination, Consent, and Verification under Representational Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17932)
*Chainarong Amornbunchornvej*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了集体反事实规划（CCP）形式化模型，揭示团队协作中表征几何的限制作用：代理人通过子空间投影感知、构想、同意和验证，存在正负二象性——跨代理接力可解锁解决方案但团队盲区目标不可验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体常完成单一成员无法独自规划、执行或验证的项目，现有模型以能力、知识或可观察性为限制因素，本文旨在提出以表征几何为核心限制的形式化框架，解释团队如何达成个体无法单独完成的目标。

Method: 建立CCP形式化模型，每个代理通过任务空间的代理特定子空间投影进行感知、构想、同意和验证；定义四个门控条件（外生实现联盟、构想、同意、任务相对验证资格）判断团队是否可达并合认识别合取目标；提出集体反事实可解性（CCS）问题，分离几何可行性、可执行达成和验证完成；设计四步穷举有界可解性方案。

Result: 揭示正负二象性：迭代跨代理接力可解锁无单次个体计划池包含的解，但依赖团队盲子空间的目标需求不可验证从而无效完成；无记忆同意与审计同意分别约束动作方向与累积轨迹状态且互不支配；有界可解性方案在 Relay闭包精确表示下可靠且完备，受限实现保持可靠但不保证完备。

Conclusion: CCP模型为序贯互促、执行者不可见目的步骤、专业边界强制子团队、以及无法有效声明的完成等团队协作现象提供了统一的几何解释，突显表征几何在集体规划中的核心作用与基本限制。

Abstract: Groups routinely complete projects that no single member can plan, execute, or verify alone. We propose a formal model of this phenomenon, Collective Counterfactual Planning (CCP), in which the binding limitation on each agent is neither capability, knowledge, nor observability, but representational geometry: each agent perceives the state, conceives moves, consents to actions, and certifies goal requirements only through a projection onto an agent-specific subspace of a common task space. Four gates jointly determine whether a team can reach a conjunctive goal and legitimately recognize that it has done so: the exogenous implementation coalitions required to perform each action, together with three representational gates -- conception, consent, and task-relative verification qualification. We define the Collective Counterfactual Solvability (CCS) problem, separating geometric feasibility, executable attainment, and validated completion. The results expose a positive-negative duality. Iterated cross-agent relay can unlock a solution that no one-shot pooling of individual plans contains, but any goal requirement depending essentially on the subspace dark to the entire team is unverifiable and therefore not validly completable, even when the trajectory accidentally attains it. Memoryless and audited consent further constrain different objects -- action directions versus cumulative trajectory states -- and neither dominates the other. A four-step exhaustive horizon-bounded solvability scheme is sound and complete under exact representation of the relay closure; restricted implementations remain sound on returned plans but need not be complete. The model gives one geometry for sequential mutual enabling, competent execution of steps whose purpose is invisible to the executor, forced sub-teaming at expertise boundaries, and completion that cannot be validly declared.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [GxP-Agent: Process-DAG Topology for Reliable Clinical Trial Programming with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16890)
*Jaime Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了GxP-Agent，一个多智能体系统，通过将监管流程编码为DAG图来解决临床试验编程中的代码生成问题。在CDISC-Bench基准测试中，该方法实现了100%结构匹配，显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验编程（将研究方案转换为符合CDISC标准的分析就绪数据集）是监管提交的瓶颈，但现有LLM代码生成方法在此任务上表现极差：使用5个前沿模型进行11次单次尝试，均未能生成有效的受试者级别分析数据集。

Method: 提出GxP-Agent多智能体系统，将监管流程顺序编码为有向无环图（DAG），将整体数据集生成分解为15个领域特定节点，由具有pharmaverse技能上下文的工作智能体执行，并包含验证门控和条件重试机制。构建了CDISC-Bench基准测试（基于FDA试点提交CDISCPilot01，包含254名受试者和49个真值ADSL变量）。

Result: GxP-Agent（使用Claude Sonnet 4.6）在三次独立运行中实现100%结构匹配（49/49变量，254条正确记录），而最佳检索增强基线为59.2%，所有单智能体和平面多智能体方法均为0%。DAG拓扑结构使较弱模型也能表现良好：GPT-4.1在相同DAG下达到59.2%平均结构匹配，而在其他架构下得分为0%。在ADAE（不良事件）任务上首次尝试即实现100%结构匹配。

Conclusion: 将领域过程知识编码为图拓扑结构——而非仅依赖LLM推理——是实现可靠、符合GxP要求的临床试验编程的关键。该方法证明了领域知识与多智能体架构结合的有效性。

Abstract: Clinical trial programming -- transforming study protocols into analysis-ready datasets under CDISC standards -- is a bottleneck in regulatory submissions, yet LLM-based code generation fails catastrophically on this task: across 11 single-shot attempts with five frontier models, none produces a valid subject-level analysis dataset. We introduce GxP-Agent, a multi-agent system that encodes regulatory process ordering as a directed acyclic graph (DAG), decomposing monolithic dataset generation into 15 domain-specific nodes executed by worker agents with pharmaverse skill context, validation gates, and conditional retry. On CDISC-Bench, a new execution-based benchmark built from the FDA pilot submission CDISCPilot01 (254 subjects, 49 ground-truth ADSL variables), GxP-Agent with Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieves 100% structural match (49/49 variables, 254 correct records) across three independent runs, compared to 59.2% for the best retrieval-augmented baseline and 0% for all single-agent and flat multi-agent approaches. The DAG topology also enables weaker models: GPT-4.1 achieves 59.2% mean structural match under the same DAG, where it scores 0% under every other architecture. The approach generalizes to ADAE (adverse events; 9-node branching DAG, 55 variables, 1,191 records), achieving 100% structural match on the first attempt. These results demonstrate that encoding domain process knowledge as graph topology -- rather than relying on LLM reasoning alone -- is a key enabler for reliable, GxP-compliant clinical trial programming.

</details>


### [108] [Runtime Governance for Agentic AI: Action-Boundary Control with Trusted Provenance and Fail-Closed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16891)
